Key ACL tests (`private::imp::tests::*`, `key_permissions` `windows::*`), `keys_inside` (`\\?\`, case, 8.3, junctions, UNC by identity), `atomic::write` share-delete, `LockFileEx` trust lock, `store_files_are_read_only`, reserved names, bundled tamper → `revoked` (e2e step 12). `fin-plugin pack` identical on Windows and Linux (`18113392…`). **Fixed:** `registry init` writes `.gitattributes` `* -text` (Git for Windows' `core.autocrlf=true` turned the signed JSON CRLF in a clone: a real bug for git-hosted registries; registries made earlier need the file by hand); the leak check in `plugins-review.test.ts` missed JSON-escaped Windows paths (its non-human assertions were vacuous there); `plugin_cmd` file URL from a `\\?\` path. Owed: macOS, a NAS and a synced-folder registry, FAT/exFAT key ring
  - [x] Windows 11 x64, Tasks 1–8 and 11 as merged (2026-11-08, `8b79f3e` + fixes on `sp8a-win`; Rust 1.99.1, MSVC 14.44 / VS 2022 Build Tools; no `nasm`, `cmake` or `go` on `PATH`): `cargo test -p fin-plugin-api -p fin-registry -p fin-plugin-cli --features fin-registry/test-http` 136 pass (Linux 134: the Windows-only tests replace the Unix ones), `packages/fin-node/test/registry.test.ts` 6 pass (`fin-node` has no native tests), clippy `-D warnings` clean; `concurrent_refreshes_share_one_store` and `crash_between_generation_and_current` 10/10. Checked: AWS-LC builds with the `cc` builder under MSVC and copies all 26 `prebuilt-nasm` objects; the trust lock is std's `File::lock` = `LockFileEx` over the whole file, per handle (a second handle in the same process waits too, as `flock` does), released when the handle closes; `atomic::write` replaces a file a reader holds open the std way (share-delete; the reader keeps the old bytes) and is refused, old bytes kept and no temp file left, when a program holds it without share-delete; generation recovery passes; re-packing the vectors' packages gives the committed bytes. Key files: nothing is set explicitly, every ACE is inherited from the folder. Under `%TEMP%`/the profile that is the owner, SYSTEM and Administrators (full); in a folder at the root of a data drive (`E:\`) it adds Authenticated Users (modify) and Users (read), so any local user can read and replace the keys: the CLI does not warn (follow-up: a protected owner-only DACL on new key folders, or a warning). `keys_inside`: `\\?\`, case, `/`, `..`, 8.3 short names, junctions and `subst` drives already resolve. Fixed: a UNC alias of a local folder (`\\localhost\C$\…`) was not inside (now also compared by folder identity, `same-file`); a missing drive lost its letter (`Q:\x` = `R:\x`); `atomic.rs` failed clippy on Windows (unused import); `feature_off_in_default_members` ran `cargo tree --offline`, which fails before a host has fetched every default member's crates (now `--locked`); `fin-workers`' test family needed `Win32_System_IO` for `WriteFile` (fin-node's `build:test` builds it alone; a workspace build hid it)
  - [x] Windows key ACL gap closed (2026-11-08, `int-8afix`, merged `sp8a-macos` 74d145e + `sp8a-win` 7e38e05; one repack test kept, `fixture_packages_repack_to_the_same_bytes`): `fin-registry` `private.rs` (+ `private_windows.rs`). New key-ring folders (and the missing folders above them) and every private key file are created owner-only in the creating call: Unix 0700/0600; Windows `CreateDirectoryW`/`CreateFileW` with a protected DACL `O:<user>D:P(A;OICI;FA;;;<user>)(A;OICI;FA;;;SY)` (files without `OICI`), nothing inherited, so a drive root's Authenticated Users/Users ACEs never reach a key, even before the rename. `SYSTEM` is granted: it is the OS and can read any file already, and services that run as it without backup semantics (VSS-based Windows Backup/File History, Defender, backup agents) would otherwise fail; Administrators are not granted. Loading is ssh-style on both OSes: `RepoBuilder::open`, every ring write and every ring key read check the ring folder, and `SigningKey::read` (ring keys, `--root-keys`, `--key`, `--new`) checks the file; anyone else reaching it is `KEY_UNPROTECTED {path, problems}` (exit 3) with `fix` = the `chmod 700`/`chmod 600` or `icacls` commands (`/setowner`, `/grant:r` user and SYSTEM, `/inheritance:r`, `/remove:g` explicit grants) and a message to rotate keys others may have read. Unix refuses any group/other bit (a `mkdir`-made 0755 ring is now refused, with `chmod 700`); Windows accepts owner = user, SYSTEM or Administrators and allow ACEs for those plus `CREATOR OWNER`/`OWNER RIGHTS` (as OpenSSH for Windows does; a ring under the profile passes), and refuses a NULL DACL (FAT/exFAT) or any other SID with non-zero access. `EditorError` gains `fix`. windows-sys 0.61 (already in the tree via tempfile) added to fin-registry with features only. Verified on Linux: `FIN_REQUIRE_SERVER=1 cargo test --workspace` 2072 pass / 229 suites, `cargo test -p fin-registry --features test-http` 100 pass, clippy `-D warnings` (workspace; and `--target x86_64-pc-windows-gnu -p fin-registry -p fin-plugin-cli --all-targets`), fmt, fin-node `build` + `build:test`, `pnpm test` 375 pass (5 skipped), `pnpm gates`, `pnpm e2e` (5 scenarios), `pnpm check:dep-age`. **Needs a Windows run** (compiled, not run): `fin-registry` lib `private::imp::tests::{fix_text_names_every_step, verbatim_names, new_folders_and_files_are_owner_only_under_a_permissive_parent, the_fix_text_fixes_it, the_profile_default_passes}` and `tests/key_permissions.rs` `windows::{a_registry_under_a_drive_root_gets_an_owner_only_key_ring, a_key_ring_others_can_reach_is_refused_until_fixed, init_refuses_an_existing_open_folder}`; also check a ring on a FAT/exFAT stick is refused with the NTFS hint. Not checked: macOS ACLs (only modes, as ssh)

## Sub-project 7: agent runtime (designed 2026-11-07)

Spec: `docs/specs/2026-11-07-sub-project-7-agent-runtime-design.md` (revision 7: 7A as built, §24; revision 2 answered an independent review, §23). Plan for the first batch: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-07-sub-project-7a-runtime-core.md`. **7A-1 runs before 5A; 7A-2 after it; 7C's policy, launcher and suites can start now.**

- [x] Owner decisions (2026-11-07, spec §20): 1 closed-model outputs MAY be training targets (against the recommendation): every example tagged with model provenance (provider, model, harness), counsel before the first training run or upload is mandatory; 2 CLI capture records the stream; 3 Linux minimum kernel 6.2+ (D31), no older-kernel fallbacks, Landlock-disabled hosts still handled (follow-up F2 below: they run everything, with warnings); 4 `auto` may merge into `main` under a per-project owner policy (off by default, human-only setting, QA gate and critic must pass, audited); 5 egress open in v1, proxy after v1, per-project API keys with spend limits; 6 the user signs in to the unmodified CLI themselves, Finisher never copies, stores or intermediates credentials (B7 reworked); 7 v1 providers: Claude Code and Codex on the user's own sign-in, the built-in agent on one OpenAI-compatible adapter (direct Anthropic and OpenAI adapters removed; follow-up F1 below adds local servers beside OpenRouter); Finisher stores only the pasted OpenRouter key
- [x] Follow-up decisions F5 (model lists discovered from the CLIs or typed for OpenRouter, the catalogue a pricing cache, local priced at zero, diagnostics by table name or keyed hash) and F6 (OpenRouter routing: every upstream by default, opt-in `data_collection: deny`/ZDR) (2026-11-07, spec §20)
- [x] Follow-up decision F1 (2026-11-07, spec §20): local models are allowed in v1 (OpenAI-compatible client with an OpenRouter preset and a local preset: Ollama, LM Studio or a user-supplied base URL; no API key for a local URL; loopback for the built-in agent only; diagnostics record the provider as `local`; training provenance `open` or `unknown`)
- [x] Owner decisions on plans 7B and 7C (2026-11-08, spec §20, plans' Decisions): 7C: `RESOLVE_NO_XDEV` off (sandbox stops mounts and hard links); one warm worker per open project, grants per project; same-project other sessions' workspaces stay readable (reach, `fin` unchanged); installer excludes `fin-workers` and `fin-sandbox-exec` from WER; no plugin-tree re-hash (residual: unsandboxed tampering); no opt-out of enforced profiles. 7B: role prompts Finisher's own (inspired by Uncut, not copied); child default budget = role's capped at half the parent's remaining per kind; saved prompts from bundle, settings and the trusted project folder; `remember: project` writes the shared `finisher.rules.json` by default with a per-user local layer and folder trust (rules combine across layers: deny beats ask beats allow, whichever layer; managed cannot be overridden; untrusted project rules do not apply); consume-first grants accepted; rollback by people and by a parent for its own children only
- [x] Follow-up decision F2 (2026-11-07, spec §8.9; also sp6 §3.5.1, sp8 §3.1): on hosts where Landlock is disabled (kernel ≥ 6.2, turned off) everything runs, with warnings: agents, hooks, media workers and plugins each show a persistent status warning (`server.status`, `fin status`) and record a diagnostics event; the `agents.unsandboxed` override is removed
- [ ] S9 spike in `archive/` (plan Task 0): seccomp io_uring/x32 on a 6.2-series kernel and a Landlock-disabled boot (B5, which now checks the warn-and-run path), macOS mach-lookup allow-list (B6), B7 the user's own CLI sign-in shared in place and never copied (per-session config dirs linking one sign-in file; in-place updates; keychain keying; Windows links without elevation), H2 Claude Code without `--bare` (`--setting-sources user`, `--strict-mcp-config`), Windows capability SIDs, init/budget-flag checks
- [ ] Auto merge needs sub-project 10: QA-gate and critic verdicts recorded on review items and bound to a snapshot (sp5 §8.5, agent §6.5); until then enabling the policy is `AUTO_MERGE_UNAVAILABLE`
- [ ] 7C must deliver the Linux Landlock `worker` profile before 6B-1 (sp6 decision 6, agent §1.4)
- [ ] 7C must grant a warm worker each job's paths (not the whole `<server-data>/jobs/` root it gets today) before warm workers are enabled under a sandbox: until then `finisherd` runs warm kinds cold whenever `caps().sandboxed` or a worker's `hello.selfSandbox` is set (owner decision, 5C-1 Task 11 review H3)

- [x] 7A-2 runtime core and the built-in agent, after 5A (plan Tasks 2, 3b, 6b, 7, 8b, 10–14; Task 15, real providers and macOS/Windows, still open under "Sub-project 7A: runtime core")

## Sub-project 7A: runtime core

Plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-07-sub-project-7a-runtime-core.md` (revision 3). Decisions after the spec (branch `sp-decisions-2`): the built-in agent's model client is one OpenAI-compatible client with two presets, OpenRouter (API key) and local (Ollama, LM Studio or a user-supplied base URL, no key; loopback allowed), recorded as provider `openrouter` or `local`; no `agents.unsandboxed` override (Landlock-disabled hosts run with persistent warnings); no direct Anthropic or OpenAI adapters in v1.

### 7A-1 (branch `sp7a1-runtime`, before 5A)

- [x] Task 1: agent kinds v1 in `fin-core`. As built: `agent.session|turn|toolCall|toolResult` at version 1 (the last three training-only), new `agent.approval` (`subject` is `userText` content); `agent.editVerdict` removed; enums `Harness`, `AgentMode` (`plan|draft|auto`), `SessionPhase`, `SessionTrigger`, `Weights`, `SignIn`, `CostBasis`, `Capture`, `Attribution`, `ApprovalKind`, `ApprovalDecision`, `DecidedBy`; provenance (`harness`, `provider`, `model`, `weights`) required meta on session and turn, `upstream?` on turns; `modelHash?` on turns as well as sessions (for `other` models); `agent.turn` content is `messagesDelta`, `output`, `system`, `tools`; `toolResult.txIds` capped at 64 (`MAX_TOOL_TX_IDS`, `EVENT_INVALID`); tables `AGENT_ROLES`, `AGENT_TOOLS`, `AGENT_PROVIDERS` gains `openrouter` (keeps `local`)
- [x] Task 3a: pure session state machine (`apps/finisherd/src/agent/session.ts`). As built: `next(state, event) → {state, effects} | {error}` over `starting|idle|running|waitingApproval|interrupted|ended`; events as planned plus `ready` (starting → idle); effects `openRun`, `closeRun`, `closeOpenToolCalls`, `expireApprovals`, `stopProvider`, `refreshCredential`, `revokeCredential`, `journal {phase}`; codes `SESSION_BUSY`, `SESSION_ENDED`, `SESSION_INTERRUPTED` (a prompt to an interrupted session); stale events (another run's end, an approval outside a run) change nothing; resume of an idle session is a no-op; open approvals expire when their run ends or is interrupted, and on resume; `wellFormed` states the invariants the fast-check properties hold (`fast-check` 4.10.2, MIT, dev only)
- [x] Task 4: model catalogue and budget ledger. As built: `catalogue/models.json` (asOf 2026-11-07, prices from OpenRouter's models API that day): built-in OpenRouter slugs, closed `anthropic/claude-sonnet-5.5`, `anthropic/claude-opus-5.5`, `anthropic/claude-haiku-5.5`, `openai/gpt-6.1-sol`, open-weight `deepseek/deepseek-v4-pro`, `moonshotai/kimi-k3`, `z-ai/glm-5.3`, `openai/gpt-oss-120b` (**the owner's list was not in the brief: the owner should confirm or replace it**); the Claude Code ids and the Codex 0.167.0 bundled slugs (`gpt-6.1-sol`, `gpt-6.1-terra`, `gpt-6-sol`, `gpt-6-luna`, `gpt-6-astra`), priced at the equivalent OpenRouter list prices; `Catalogue` (`load`/`parse` → `CATALOGUE_INVALID`, `get`, `wireModel` with `variant`, `cacheReadWeight`, `price` → `reported|priced|unknown`, rounded up); `provider` also takes `local` and `agents.models` entries take `provider: openrouter|local` (a local model is priced at zero); `Usage.input` excludes cache reads and writes, `reasoning` is inside `output`; `ledger.ts` (`LedgerStore`, `MemoryLedger`, kinds `tokens|money|wallMs`, `utcDay`); `budget.ts` (`Budget` with `startRun`/`endRun`, `canCall`, `canTool`, `charge`, `chargeTool`, `check`, `raise`, `readings`; options `price` and `cacheReadWeight`; per-run caps refuse the next call but never stop the session); `clock.ts` (`Clock`, `manualClock`, `Ids`, `ulidAt`); `AGENT_MODELS` = the catalogue ids + `other` (drift test `catalogue_ids_in_agent_models`)
- [x] Task 5: `SecretStore`, `SecretBox` seam, `ModelGateway`. As built: `secrets/box.ts` (`Sealed {v: 1, data}`, `SecretBox`, `identityBox`, `sealedBytes`); `secrets/store.ts` (`FileSecretStore`: `<server-data>/secrets.json` 0600 in a 0700 directory, entries `{id, provider: openrouter, label, projectId?, sealed, createdAt}`; `put` and `remove` are async and serialised through one queue, temp file created 0600 then renamed; `put` replaces the key bound to the same project (or the server default); `list` never returns a value; `reveal` (the gateway's only) opens through the box; codes `PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE` (any provider but `openrouter`), `SECRET_INVALID` (label, project id, key not 8–1024 printable bytes), `SECRET_CORRUPT`, `SECRET_NOT_FOUND`); `secrets/gateway.ts` (`ModelGateway.withKey(projectId, sessionId, f)`: project key else server default, `PROVIDER_AUTH` for no key or a session not running; `AuditSink` records the first grant per session and every refusal; the buffer is zero-filled when `f` settles; an error from `f` that quotes the key is replaced (`FinError` keeps its code, details dropped); `hasKey`, `forget`)
- [x] Task 6a: adapter contract, `ProviderEvent` schema, fake provider. As built: `@finisher/contracts` `agent-events.ts` (`Harness`, `Mode`, `SessionStatus`, `NodeKind`, `BudgetSpec`, `ContentBlock`, `ErrorShape`, `ProviderEvent` with the spec's 15 types plus `unknown {name}`, `PROVIDER_EVENT_TYPES`); `agent/adapter.ts` (`ProviderAdapter`, `ProviderSession` with `instance` and `events: Stream<BoundEvent {instance, event}>`, `SessionSpec` (no budget facts: `TurnInput.facts` carries them), `RuntimeAnswer` adds `toolResult`, `Capabilities`, `Detection`, `toProviderEvent`: unknown type → `unknown`, a known type that does not decode → `PROVIDER_PROTOCOL`); `agent/fake.ts` (`FakeAdapter(scripts)` with steps `emit`, `awaitTool`, `awaitAnswer`, `crash`, `late`, `spawnFails`; auto `turnStarted`/`turnCompleted`; a replaced instance flushes its late events bound to itself; `log` for tests); `session.ts` now takes `SessionStatus` from contracts; `test/support/adapter-contract.ts` (`adapterContractSuite`, `oneTurn`)
- [x] Task 8a: `FIN_CALL` as the default idempotency key in `fin`. As built: `crates/fin/src/agent_env.rs` (`command_name` from clap's subcommand chain = the registry name, checked for every registry command; `default_idem_key(cmd, explicit, call)`; `apply` fills `Globals.idem_key` from `FIN_CALL` for a registry write without `--idem-key`, so edits, history writes and `project create` all key on it); `main.rs` calls it once after parsing; a malformed `FIN_CALL` is `USAGE` for a write and ignored for a read. **Deviation:** no `<FIN_CALL>.<line>` suffix: a `batch` is one transaction, so its key is `FIN_CALL` itself (a merge's group records are already `<txId>-<n>`). The test support clears `FIN_CALL`
- [x] Task 9: OpenAI-compatible model client (OpenRouter and local presets) and the fake model server. As built: `builtin/request.ts` (neutral `ModelRequest`, `ModelMessage` user/assistant/tool with opaque `reasoning` details, `ToolDef`; `chatBody(req, {preset, caching})` in a fixed key order: function tools, `max_tokens`, `stream`, `stream_options.include_usage`; OpenRouter only: `reasoning {effort|max_tokens}` and, for `caching: explicit`, `cache_control: ephemeral` on the system block and on the last message); `builtin/compat.ts` (`openAICompatibleClient`, `openrouterClient`, `localClient`; the `openai` SDK built per request with the key from a `KeySource`, `maxRetries: 0`, logging off, `x-stainless-*` headers stripped, no `Authorization` for `local`; the bytes sent are captured from the SDK's `fetch` and `bodyHash` is their BLAKE3; `ModelStreamEvent` `textDelta|reasoningDelta|retry|done`; `ModelResult` with tool calls assembled by index, reasoning details rebuilt by `(type, index)`, `Usage.input` = prompt − cache reads − cache writes, `costMicros` from OpenRouter's `usage.cost`, `upstream` from the chunk's `provider`, `capture {request, response chunks}`, latency and TTFT; retries on 408/429/5xx, connection failures, a stream cut short and an error chunk in a 200 stream: `retry-after` (seconds or date) else 1 s × 2ⁿ ≤ 60 s, 5 attempts, a `retry` event each; codes `PROVIDER_AUTH` (401/403, 402 credits), `PROVIDER_RATE_LIMITED {retryAfterMs}`, `PROVIDER_OVERLOADED`, `MODEL_CONTEXT_EXCEEDED`, `MODEL_UNKNOWN` (404), `PROVIDER_PROTOCOL`, `SESSION_INTERRUPTED` on abort; `checkBaseUrl`: https, or http on loopback, and the local preset also http elsewhere (no key is sent)); `agent/blake3.ts` (TS BLAKE3 hash mode, checked against the official vectors: Node has none and no dependency was added); `test/support/fake-model-server.ts` and seven SSE fixtures; `openai` (Apache-2.0) pinned at 7.47.0 until 7.53.0 is ≥7 days old

- [x] Review fixes for 7A-1 and the follow-up decisions F5 (models discovered or typed; the catalogue a pricing and metadata cache) and F6 (every OpenRouter upstream by default; opt-in `data_collection: deny`/ZDR), recorded in spec revision 5. As built:
  - **B1** `FIN_CALL` is the default idempotency key only with `FIN_CALL_IDEM=1` (the built-in `fin` tool's single-shot marker); a shell's several `fin`s under one call all commit; `FIN_CALL` stays in the actor header
  - **B2** every failed attempt reports its cost (`retry`/`failed` events carry the generation id and reported or estimated usage); no retry once more than 512 characters streamed; `settleGeneration` (`GET /generation`); `Budget.charge(…, {call: false})` for attempts
  - **H1** `AGENT_UPSTREAMS` (OpenRouter's 120 provider slugs) and `agent_upstream` (display name → slug), unknown upstreams `upstreamHash`; **H2** `AGENT_MODELS` a naming table, catalogue ⊆ table, every agent table append-only (`tests/fixtures/agent_tables.snapshot.json`); the agent kinds are wire kinds (`wire_agent.rs`): raw model ids → name or `other` + `modelHash`, unkeyed `blake3:` hashes keyed
  - **H3** `canCall` reserves projected money (session and day) and `charge` settles it; `release`; **H4** an approval opened outside a run is expired at once; properties: approvals leave only by answer or expiry, every `openRun` has one `closeRun`
  - **M1** the openrouter preset is pinned to `https://openrouter.ai/api/v1` (loopback only through `testBaseUrl`); the local preset warns on plain http off-machine and sends the `finisher-local` placeholder bearer; **M2** the secret store checks and repairs owner and modes at every read and write, refuses links, locks across processes (`secrets.lock`), fsyncs the directory, on Windows the owner-only DACL of 5A's `makePrivateDir`, read back with `checkPrivateDir` (was `SECRET_STORE_UNAVAILABLE` until the Windows run of 2026-11-08); **M3** `blake3Hex`/`blake3KeyedHex` from fin-node, the TS copy deleted, `bodyHash` keyed when journaled; **M4** a 60 s idle timer between chunks
  - **L1** zero-fill comment corrected, the key scrubbed along `err.cause`; **L2** abort listeners `once` and removed; **L3** `retry-after` honoured up to 10 min, a longer one fails `PROVIDER_RATE_LIMITED`; **L4** per-run counters and notices reset by design (documented)
  - F5: `models.json` holds presets, defaults and families only (no model list); `prices.ts` caches OpenRouter's models API; `cli-models.ts` parses Codex `model/list` (paged) and Claude Code's `initialize` models; local models priced at zero. F6: `Routing` → `provider.{data_collection, zdr}` only when set
  - Lockfile: crc32fast, flate2, zip and ureq held a patch back for this machine's 7-day registry proxy (drop once the integration branch's versions pass it)
  - [ ] Bump `openai` from 7.47.0 to 7.53.0 (`apps/finisherd/package.json`, `pnpm-lock.yaml`, `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md`) once 7.53.0 is ≥7 days old; `pnpm check:npm-age` reports when a locked package is too young.

### 7A-2 (after 5A)

- [x] Task 2: contracts for the agent RPCs and their scopes (branch `sp7a2-t2`). As built:
  - `@finisher/contracts` `agent.ts`: `Session` (with `weights`, `usage`, `rootBranch?` absent in `plan`, no `providerRef`), `Run`, `Node` (inline `payload` or `blobRef`, `callId`), `Approval`, `WatchItem` (`nodeAdded|delta|statusChanged|usageUpdated|approvalOpened`), `Capabilities`/`ProviderInfo` for `agent.providers` (a key set or not, the local preset and reachability, never a key or an address), `AgentSelfSuccess`/`AgentSelfTryPayload` (Decision 14a: `record|setDefault|clear`, each try keeps the default it replaced), and the 15 payload/success pairs; bounded text (200,000 chars), title (200), attachments (8), list pages (≤200)
  - `FinRpcs` gains `agent.session.start|prompt|steer|interrupt|resume|end|list|get|delete`, `agent.watch` (stream, in `STREAM_RPCS`), `agent.approval.list|answer`, `agent.providers`, `agent.self`, `agent.self.try`; `RPC_METHODS` (now in `fin-core` `events/names_rpc.rs`, re-exported from `names.rs` to keep it under 600 lines) lists all 15 and the two drift tests pass
  - scopes `agent.read`, `agent.run`, `agent.approve` and `agent.admin`; the last two are `HUMAN_ONLY` (so `local` and `agent` are refused, the console needs the typed code: both payloads carry `challenge`). `agent.run` and `agent.read` are not human-only: `fin` drives sessions as `local`. A start needs `agent.run`, plus `project.read` and `project.write` unless `mode: plan`
  - new rule field `kinds`: the principal kinds that may call an RPC whatever they hold, else `SCOPE_DENIED {reason: principalKind}` (audited as `auth.denied`, before the human-only check). `agent.self` and `agent.self.try` allow `agent` only (scopes `project.read`/`project.write`); the session-driving RPCs (`agent.session.start|prompt|steer|interrupt|resume|end`) allow `local`, `console` and `device`, so an agent credential cannot start or drive a session in 7A even if minted with `agent.run` (7B relaxes it for a parent's own children)
  - `apps/finisherd/src/agent/rpc.ts` is a placeholder: every agent call that passes the chain answers `SERVER_UNAVAILABLE {reason: agentRuntimeNotWired}`; **Task 12 replaces it**. Handlers must check a session id against the principal's project reach (the chain cannot: the payload names no project), and the subtree check of `agent.self.try`'s `branch` (Task 7)

- [x] Task 6b: the runtime engine (branch `sp7a2-t6b`, on 3b with 7 merged). As built:
  - `agent/runtime.ts` `Runtime` (commands; `makeRuntime(deps)` takes the core from `AgentCoreTag`) over `agent/runtime-engine.ts` (`RuntimeEngine`: per-session order, events → nodes, budgets, outbox, instances), `runtime-live.ts` (one open provider instance: scope, pump, calls in flight, pending stop, held steers, reservations), `runtime-deps.ts` (`SessionPlanner`, `SessionEnvironment`, `ToolExecutor`/`ToolCall`, `RuntimeJournal` + `NO_JOURNAL`, `Pricing` + `REPORTED_PRICING`, `limitsOf`, `runFacts`), `watch-hub.ts` (`agent.watch` fan-out)
  - deps: `core` (Task 7's `AgentCore`: the one store and `SessionCredentials`), `registry` (`BranchRegistry`: heads and `openSession`'s ops), `adapters`, `planner`, `environment`, `tools?` (per harness: the harnesses whose calls the runtime runs), `journal?`, `pricing?`, `defaults?`, `retentionDays?`, `approvalTtlMs?` (7 days), `timers?`, `log?`
  - `boot()` (before the transports listen): `recoverAfterCrash`, `sweepOrphans(0)`, the outbox drained; then the hourly `sweep(now)` and a 5 s wall-clock budget check. `close()`; `abandon()` (a crash, for tests); `stats()` (`live`, `dropped`)
  - commands, each in the session's promise queue after the events and commands before it: `start(req, by, meta)` (planner = Decision 15.1; `openSession` with `registryBranchOps(registry, meta)`, `meta` = `{...actorFor(by, headers), guard}`; then the first run), `prompt` (`BUDGET_EXCEEDED` when a session budget is used up; `headAtStart` from the registry), `steer` (provider with `steer`: a `userMessage` node and `provider.steer`, `{queued: false}`; without: held, the next run with trigger `steer`, `{queued: true}`; idle: a `steer` run), `interrupt` (`userInterrupt`; the branch stays), `resume(id, by, prompt?)` (`refreshForSession` first, so an ended credential refuses it; then `resumed`, and a run when `prompt` is given), `end` (`userEnded`), `delete` (ends, journals the end, then `store.delete`), `approve(id, decision, add, by)` (a `budget` approval: raises the limits, consumes it and resumes), `watch(id, fromSeq?)` (stored nodes, the status, then live items; ends at `ended`)
  - events: an instance opens at a session's first run (its `providerRef` saved, `SessionSpec.env` gets `FIN_CREDENTIAL = credentialPath(store.root, id)`) and stays while `idle`; events of any other instance are dropped (an exited instance keeps its pump until its stream ends, so late events are dropped, not lost); `message`/`reasoning`/`plan`/`usage` → nodes, deltas → watch items only, `unknown` → `notice {unknownEvent}`, `capture` → `journal.capture`; `toolStarted` → a ULID call id and its `toolCall` node **before** anything runs it; for a harness in `tools` the runtime runs it off the queue and answers `respond(providerId, {kind: toolResult})`; the `toolResult` node comes from the adapter's `toolCompleted`; a provider `request` is declined by policy (an `approval` node; 7B answers them); `turnFailed` → `error` node and `interrupted` (`MODEL_CONTEXT_EXCEEDED` ends the run only); `exited` (or a stream that ends) → `error` + `interrupted {PROVIDER_EXITED}`; a spawn failure fails the prompt with `PROVIDER_EXITED`
  - budgets: `usage` priced (`Pricing`: reported cost, local zero, else `unknown`), charged, added to run and session; tool calls counted; 80 % notices as `notice` nodes; a stop waits for the calls in flight (the safe point), then an `error` node, `interrupted {BUDGET_EXCEEDED}` and a `budget` approval (`subject {kind, used, limit}`); the per-run tool cap and a pending stop refuse new runtime-run calls with a `BUDGET_EXCEEDED` result. **`SessionSpec.gate`** (new, `adapter.ts` `CallGate`): `beforeCall(projected)` reserves money until the next `usage` event settles it (`release()` for an attempt without one); a refusal stops the session, except the per-run model-call cap, which ends only the call; a replaced instance's gate refuses
  - outbox: drained at boot and after every `apply`; credential effects go to `core.credentials.handle`, the journal phases and synthetic results to `RuntimeJournal`; a failing item holds its session's later ones until the next drain
  - codes: `BUDGET_EXCEEDED` (7), `HARNESS_UNKNOWN`, `APPROVAL_NOT_FOUND` (3), `SESSION_BUSY`, `SESSION_ENDED` (4), `SESSION_INTERRUPTED`, `PROVIDER_EXITED` (5) in `output.rs` and `console/exit-codes.ts`; `AgentStore` gains `activeRun`, `lastSeq`, `approval(id)`
  - tests `agent-runtime.test.ts` (25, over Task 7's real core and a fake registry): the plan's eleven, `resume_after_crash_never_reruns_tool` at four crash points (after the announce, with the `fin` process outliving the server, after the commit, after the result) plus a SIGKILLed child server, and the gate, the wall clock, an over-budget prompt, turn failures, declined requests, boot, a failing outbox item, a refused resume, plan mode, delete
  - **Deviations:** the runtime takes `core` and a `registry` rather than an Effect layer of its own; `start` takes the call's `meta`; `resume` takes an optional prompt; `approve` is a runtime method (it owns the budgets); in `resume_after_crash_never_reruns_tool` the commit count is 1 wherever the commit landed and 0 for a crash right after the announce (the tool never ran; nothing re-runs it); deltas are not throttled to 20 Hz yet; approvals are not expired at `expiresAt` (not 6b's in the plan: a sweep needs a store method that also leaves the session's open list)
  - **For 8b:** the built-in adapter is registered with its executor in `tools.builtin` (`ToolCall` carries `callId` = `FIN_CALL`, `cwd`, `env`); emit `toolStarted` only for calls that may run now (the runtime runs each as it is announced: serial writes are the loop's order); call `spec.gate.beforeCall` before each model call and end the run on a refusal; emit one `usage` per attempt (or `gate.release()`); `capabilities.steer: true` if the loop queues steers itself
  - **For 10:** implement `RuntimeJournal` (`session` from the outbox at least once, `session` may be undefined after a delete; `turn` per `usage`; `capture`; `toolCall`/`toolResult` with `synthetic`; `approval`); calls are best effort (a throw is logged)
  - **For 11:** read `session_txs` before `Runtime.delete` (it removes them) (superseded by the 3b/6b/7 review: the rows outlive the session)
  - **For 12:** build the runtime with `makeRuntime` after the core, call `boot()` before the transports listen and `close()` on shutdown; implement `SessionPlanner` (catalogue, roles, key and endpoint checks) and `SessionEnvironment` (role prompt + `fin` map, `workspace/<id>/`, `childEnv`); pass `{...actorFor(by, headers), guard}` to `start`; convert `agent.watch`'s `fromNode` to a seq; register `fake` only with test hooks
- [x] Review fixes for Tasks 3b, 6b and 7 (branch `sp7a2-t6b-fix`, on 6b with `sp4c-edit-breadth` merged), recorded in spec revision 6. As built:
  - **revocations are never lost:** `SessionCredentials.handle` throws `CONTROL_DB_BUSY` while the credential's end is in memory only (`Credentials.endUnsaved(id)`), so the `revokeCredential` item stays queued and the next drain writes it; `drain` holds that session's later items and carries on with the others; `reconcile()` (in `main.ts` after the start drain, and in `Runtime.boot`) revokes every live session credential (an `agent` one labelled `agent session (<role>)`, no absolute expiry) whose session has ended or is gone. The runtime test core now passes `persist`, as `main.ts` does
  - **approvals expire:** `answerApproval` past `expiresAt` expires it, drops it from the session's open list (new event `approvalExpired`) and throws `APPROVAL_EXPIRED` (exit 4, `output.rs`, `exit-codes.ts`); `AgentStore.expireOverdueApprovals(now)`, run first by `Runtime.sweep` (hourly)
  - **budgets fail closed:** `Pricing.known(provider, model)`; `Runtime.start` refuses a non-local model without a price with `PRICING_UNKNOWN` (exit 5) before anything is made (`REPORTED_PRICING` knows only local models; `cataloguePricing(catalogue)` is the real one); `Reservation` holds `tokens` too, checked as `used + reserved`; a non-local usage with basis `unknown` is charged at least its reservation's projection
  - **`agent.watch` is bounded:** `WatchHub.watch(source, id, fromSeq, bufferSize = 1000)`; a watcher holding `bufferSize` live items beyond its unread snapshot is ended `WATCH_LAGGING {sessionId, lastSeq, bufferSize}` (exit 5); `RuntimeDeps.watchBufferSize`
  - **session reach for the RPCs:** `agent/session-access.ts` `sessionInReach(store, principal, id)` / `approvalInReach` (out of reach = `SESSION_NOT_FOUND` / `APPROVAL_NOT_FOUND`, word for word the missing ones) and `projectsFilter(principal)` for `list`
  - **crash recovery returns reservations:** `recoverAfterCrash` zeroes `budgets.reserved` and `budget_days.reserved` first
  - **a budget stop has a deadline:** `RuntimeDeps.stopGraceMs` (30 s); then the running calls are aborted (`AbortSignal`) and the stop goes on (`Live.stopDeadline`, cleared by the stop and `closeLive`)
  - **no re-mint of an ended credential:** agent migration **0002** adds `sessions.readyAt` (set at `ready`; existing rows backfilled as ready) and `session_txs.projectId`; `refreshForSession` mints only for a session that never reached `idle` (`AgentStore.everReady`), else `CREDENTIAL_EXPIRED`; a re-mint uses the starter kind's default scopes
  - **random temp name** for `credential.json` (a stale `*.tmp` never blocks a write)
  - **sessions stay in their branches:** `SESSION_REFUSED_RPCS` in `reach.ts` (the chain hook): `project.open|create`, `project.acknowledgeSalvage`, `diagnostics.project|consent|purge` → `SCOPE_DENIED {reason: agentSession}`; `MODE_SCOPES` keeps `diagnostics.read`
  - **starter scopes:** a non-`local` starter's scopes are intersected with the mode's
  - **owner decision, `session_txs` retention:** `delete` and the retention sweep keep the rows; `AgentStore.purgeTxs({projectId} | {sessionId})` (with the project, or an admin purge); spec §3.3, plan Decision 4
  - **owner decision, no sandbox before 7C:** `agent/warnings.ts` `SANDBOX_DISABLED {profile: agent, os, reason: before7C}`; in `Runtime.start`'s answer (contracts `AgentSessionStartSuccess.warnings?`, `AgentWarning`), `AgentProvidersSuccess.warnings?`, `server.status.warnings` and the server log at start; spec §8.9 records that before 7C a tool can authenticate as `local` via `local.key`
  - split for the 600-line cap: the outbox delivery moved to `agent/runtime-outbox.ts` (`OutboxDrainer`); `store-rows.ts` gains `sessionInsertArgs`, `sessionListQuery`, `approvalExpired`
  - tests: `agent-review.test.ts` (16) and `agent-review-server.test.ts` (3), one or more per finding; `agent-store.test.ts` (format 2, an approval that has not expired, `session_txs` kept), `agent-budget.test.ts` (token reservations) updated
  - **For 12:** every `agent.session.*`, `agent.watch` and `agent.approval.*` handler resolves its session through `sessionInReach`/`approvalInReach` (lists: `projectsFilter`); build the runtime with `pricing: cataloguePricing(catalogue)`; answer `agent.providers` with `warnings: agentWarnings()` and pass `start`'s `warnings` through; convert `WATCH_LAGGING`'s `lastSeq` for the client (`fromNode`); a project removal (when one exists) calls `store.purgeTxs({projectId})`
- [x] Task 8b: the built-in loop and its tools; `fin` under an agent credential (branch `sp7a2-t8b`, on 6b). As built:
  - `agent/builtin/loop.ts` `BuiltinAdapter({client: (preset) => ModelClient, gateway?, catalogue, finPath, index?, nodes?, limits?})`: per turn the facts + prompt as one user message, then model call → tools → results → next call until `endTurn`; reads announced together, each write alone after everything before it has its result (`isWrite`: `workspace_write`, and `fin` commands the index marks as writes or does not know; `--dry-run` is a read); `spec.gate.beforeCall` before every attempt (a retry included), one `usage` per attempt or `gate.release()`; a `modelCalls` refusal ends the run (`turnCompleted {modelCallCap}`), any other fails the turn; per-run caps 60 model calls / 200 tool calls (`toolCallCap`); tool results to the model ≤ 16 KiB (head 12 + note + tail 4); context stop at 85 % of the window (`MODEL_CONTEXT_EXCEEDED`, run only); steers join before the next call; interrupt aborts the stream and the waits (`turnCompleted {interrupted}`); `fin view` gets `--budget` = min(4000, 5 % of the window); a model without tools is `MODEL_UNKNOWN {noTools}` at open; on reopen the conversation is rebuilt from the session's nodes (`loop-history.ts` `historyFromNodes`), synthetic results included, so no tool re-runs

  - **fin-node:** `kindVersion(name, source)` (`src/kinds.rs`): the newest non-reserved version a source may emit, else `null`; `journalVerdicts(journal, lookup)` is the `VerdictSink` over the diagnostics journal (`source: agent`)

The release `finisherd` and `fin`; a scripted Chat Completions server on loopback chosen by the conversation (prompt + tool results seen), driven through the **local preset** (`agent.local.set <url>`, `agent start --provider local`): no key, no network, no test hooks

(3) two commits by `agent:editor:<id>` on the session branch (`tx.get`, `session_txs`), `main`'s content untouched, `--branch main` is `BRANCH_NOT_ALLOWED` (7), a write with no `--branch` lands on the session branch after a person's `fin try` (and `agent.self` still says the root), `caption import --words /etc/hosts` is `PATH_NOT_ALLOWED` (7), the B1 shape `caption --project / import …` is `PROJECT_NOT_FOUND` (3), a missing credential file is `UNAUTHENTICATED`, an ended session's credential is `CREDENTIAL_REVOKED` with `FIN_SERVER_DATA` on offer (no fallback to `local`); (4) exports with training on: `agent.toolResult.txIds` ↔ `log.commit` for every session tx (keyed ids), trace turns are deltas (`from > 0` after the first) whose `replayBodies` equal the exact bodies the model server received, provenance (`provider: local`, `weights`) on every trace record; (5) `SIGKILL` once the third tool call of session C is announced and unanswered (read from `control.db`), restart → `interrupted {serverRestart}`, `agent resume` + `agent prompt`, one result for the third call, the commit count and the imported SRT unchanged, no call id twice; (6) `fin merge` as `local`: every session tx `merged` (`session_tx_outcomes` `via: merge, by: user`); (7) a token budget (250) stops session D `BUDGET_EXCEEDED` with a `budget` approval; `agent.approval.answer {add: {tokens}}` resumes it to `idle` and a prompt then finishes; (8) a second server, training off, no key, a directory where `secrets.json` would be: the local session completes, no `agent-traces/`, no content in `events/`, turn metadata `provider: local`, `weights` open, cost 0, `model: other` + keyed `modelHash` (the raw id is in no record), no record holds the model server's URL; (9) leak grep over both servers' files, every `fin`/console output and every request the model server saw (dummy key, credential keys, `local.key`, `console.key`), with a planted-key control; `agent watch` renders nodes and exits `0` at `ended`

  - a rule `ask` for an agent in a running session writes a card (30 min) and answers `APPROVAL_PENDING` (exit 8); people, session-less agent credentials and idle sessions keep 5A's `APPROVAL_REQUIRED` (exit 7); a dry run passes an `ask` (a `deny` still refuses it)
  - identical `(session, command, argsHash)` reuses the open card; 16 per session and 256 per server (open, not yet due, `rule`/`provider` kinds) then `APPROVAL_LIMIT` (exit 4), never dropping one
  - `argsHash` is HMAC-SHA256 with `<server-data>/auth/approval.key` (never rotated) over the payload's declared fields and `tx`'s fin-core fields (no `txId`, `author`, `idemKey`, client `argsHash`, `via`); `baseRev` included; ops are hashed as sent (no TS op canonicaliser: a junk key inside an op shows on the card)
  - card args capped at 64 KiB for display (`truncated`), the hash covers the full args; `APPROVAL_PENDING` audited as `auth.denied` with outcome `pending` and the card id
  - a `waitingApproval` session keeps its model key (the built-in loop's next call)
- [x] Task 5: one-time grants, waits, answers, `remember` and provider requests (`agent/{permissions,provider-requests,remember,approvals-rpc}.ts`, `agent/builtin/approval-wait.ts`, agent migration 0005 `approvals.grantIdem`, `fin approval wait|ls`)
  - a rule `ask` first consumes an unconsumed `allow` card for exactly `(session, command, argsHash)` (one SQL statement) before the handler; a typed handler failure other than `INTERNAL`/`ENGINE_CLOSED` restores it; a crash loses it (owner-accepted); restore key: `idemKey`, `txId`, `FIN_CALL`, fresh id
  - `agent.approval.wait` (`agent.read`, a session's own cards; ≤ 90 s, 4 concurrent waits per card's session, abort on disconnect); sessions now hold `agent.read` in every mode, the other `agent.read` RPCs stay people's (`SESSION_PEOPLE_READS`)
  - `remember`: `session`/`projectLocal` add a local rule (source `approval:<id>`), `project` appends to `finisher.rules.json` (atomic, regular files only, mode kept; trust follows only from the previous hash); a failed answer removes only its own rule
  - **owner decision (2026-11-08), implemented:** an exact remembered `allow` (the card's command, no glob or `where`, its session/project scope holding) lifts a matching `ask` from the local, project (trusted), user and role layers, per name of the call; never a `deny`, never a managed `ask`; other allows keep deny > ask > allow. **Per project:** it lifts only asks bound to its own project (a project file's, a local rule's of the project or of a session in it); an unbound ask (user, role, all-project local) only when every project the call names has its own remembered allow; a `session` one only for that session. Proof is the server's record (`agent_remembered`, agent migration 0006: written by `remember` only and only for an `allow`, with its effect, `proves` requiring `effect = 'allow'`; a local rule in one transaction with its row, a project-file rule keyed by project and id), never the `approval:<id>` source; trusting a project (`trustProject`) drops the records of remembered rules the file no longer holds with the same id, effect and command (no `where`), so a remembered deny edited into an allow, or a rule removed and written back by hand, lifts nothing. `agent.rules.list` marks proven remembered allows `remembered: true` and the console prints `[remembered: lifts a matching ask]`. The "has no effect" notice is gone. Tests: `remembered_allow_lifts_ask`, pin-exactness and per-project units, `agent-remember-lift.test.ts` (12, incl. forged sources, deny flip, stale record, two projects through the server's layers: no RPC names two projects for an agent today); 13 mutants (managed, deny, source-only, no proof, not exact, cross-project, glob pin, no project bound, no session bound, proves without effect, deny recorded (project, local), no trust prune) each caught
  - provider requests: tool deny → deny; `plan` write outside `workspace/<session>/` (textual check; symlinks and case are 7C's) → deny; tool ask → `provider` card (timer at `expiresAt`, answer through `respond`); else allow; inputs over 1 MiB are never allowed
  - the built-in tool runner waits on a pending card (`waitingApproval`) and re-runs the call with the same `FIN_CALL` after `allow`

Plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-08-sub-project-7c-os-sandbox.md`. Batch 7C-1 (Tasks 1–3) must land before 6B-1.

- [x] Task 1: `fin-sandbox` policy, profiles and root enumeration (branch `sp7c-t1`). As built: `policy.rs` (`SandboxPolicy` of spec §8.1, `Profile`, `Os`, `ReadPolicy::Only` and, under `cfg(test)` or `test-hooks`, `Everything {except}`; `Net::{Open {loopback}, Loopback {ports}, None}`; `DeviceRule`; `Limits`; `validate` refuses relative and unnormalised paths, a write root at or under a protected root, a path both granted and denied, and each profile's disallowed rules (IP for `hook`/`worker`/`plugin` beyond a `hook`'s loopback ports, sockets for `worker`/`plugin`, devices for `agent`/`hook`, `worker` processes ≠ 1, a 0 limit or port, a `mach` name outside `[A-Za-z0-9._-]`); the reference model `readable`/`writable`: the most specific root wins, a tie denies, a root inside a denied root is a hole (the session's files in server data), and a directory holding an exclusion is not readable or writable as a whole); `enumerate.rs` (`enumerate(policy, &dyn Tree)`, `write_roots`/`read_roots(…, tree)`: expands a root around the exclusions inside it, never grants a link, junction or special file met while expanding, sorted and minimal; `Fs` lister; proptest `enumerate_props` against the model, 1,024 cases per run, 30,000 checked once; mutants "grant links" and "drop `protect`" fail it); `inputs.rs` (`AgentInput`, `HookInput {fin?: HookFin}`, `WorkerInput {scope: cold {jobDir, sources} | warm {workerDir, libraryRoots}}`, `PluginInput`, `FinEndpoint {tcp | unixSocket | pipe}`, in `@finisher/core-types` through `export_schema`, `Profile` as `SandboxProfile`; the policy is not exported); `profile/{agent,hook,worker,plugin}.rs` (owner decisions: other sessions' workspaces of the project readable, `finisher.rules.json` protected; layout checked: `workspace/<sessionId>`, `workspace/shared`, `agents/<sessionId>`, the store inside `providers/<harness>/home/`, a warm worker's directory strictly inside `jobs/`, a plugin's store directory inside `plugins/store/`, toolchains and project outside server data and the sensitive home paths); `data.rs` and `data/<os>/toolchain-base.txt` (placeholders until Task 0). Deviations: `Net::Open` carries the loopback ports (the agent listener's port, Decision 13); enumeration takes a `Tree` and returns `Result`; the worker input is a `cold | warm` enum; `PluginInput` has no store root (it is `<serverData>/plugins/store`). Tests: `policy.rs` 5, `enumerate_props.rs` 5, `profiles.rs` 12, unit 4, `generated.test.ts` +1
- [x] Task 2: Linux Landlock and the `worker` profile's Landlock phase (branch `sp7c-t2`). As built: `linux/landlock.rs` (`query()`: the version query is the only status source, `EPERM`/`ENOSYS`/`EOPNOTSUPP` → `Support::Disabled`, anything else not understood → `Unavailable`; `ruleset(policy, Abi)` enumerates against the real filesystem and builds one `HardRequirement` ruleset with every right of the ABI handled: read roots `READ_FILE|READ_DIR` (+`EXECUTE` except `worker`), write roots + `WRITE_FILE|TRUNCATE|REMOVE_*|MAKE_DIR|MAKE_REG|MAKE_SYM|REFER` (+`MAKE_SOCK|MAKE_FIFO` for `agent`/`hook`, +`EXECUTE` for `agent`), devices `READ_FILE|READ_DIR` (+`WRITE_FILE|IOCTL_DEV` read-write), `RESOLVE_UNIX` on `unix_sockets` at ABI ≥ 9, TCP `connect` to the policy's ports at ABI ≥ 4 (`bind` never; `agent` handles `connect` only, 80/443 + loopback ports), abstract-socket and signal scoping at ABI ≥ 6; roots opened `O_PATH|O_NOFOLLOW`, a link refused, a missing root skipped; ABI < 3 → `Unavailable {need: "kernel ≥ 6.2"}`); `linux/apply.rs` (`restrict_self[_at]`: `TSYNC` at ABI ≥ 8, below it the process must have one thread (`ApplyError::Threads`); anything short of full enforcement and `no_new_privs` is an error); `linux/gpu.rs` + `data/linux/gpu.txt` (the worker's base GPU rows: render nodes read-write, `/dev/dri`, Vulkan ICD/layer files, `drirc.d`, `ld.so.cache`, `/usr/lib*`, `/sys/class/drm`, `/sys/dev/char`, `/sys/devices/system/cpu`, canonicalised, measured with strace for lavapipe); `self_apply.rs` (`worker` only: `WorkerInput` → `profile::worker` → Landlock; any other profile, an unreadable input or a failure is `Err` → exit 78 before `hello`; not Linux: `Err` until Task 8); `ApplyError` in `error.rs`; `bin/fin-sandbox-probe.rs` (`required-features = ["test-hooks"]`, Linux only). `fin-workers` registers `fin_sandbox::self_apply` first thing in `main`. Two changes so the profile allows exactly what the workers need: `fin-library`'s Linux opener opens the root `O_PATH` (it never lists it), so a media source granted as one file opens; the `library` family holds `/proc/self/mountinfo` open from before the sandbox (`volume::linux::hold_mountinfo`, re-read from offset 0, so never stale). Deviations: the S8 cases are JSON (`tests/cases/files.json`; no `toml` crate in the tree); `self_apply_fails_closed` runs the real `fin-workers` binary (`crates/fin-workers/tests/self_sandbox_linux.rs`); a Landlock-disabled host fails the self-sandbox closed until Task 3 adds F2's run-with-warning. Mutants: granting the policy's raw write roots instead of the enumerated ones fails 16 cases at ABI 3, 4 and 8 (hard links and renames of protected and denied files, writes in them); widening `in/` to write fails 5 cases of `worker_writes_only_out_and_tmp`; the old `O_RDONLY` opener root makes the sandboxed `media.look` report `ORIGINALS_MISSING` (`EACCES`). Recorded: `REFER` on read roots alone flips nothing (every link or rename needs `MAKE_*` at the destination and the kernel refuses a move that gains rights). Tests: `linux_landlock.rs` 10 (49 cases × ABI 3/4/kernel, each denied case also run unsandboxed as a control), `escape_worker_linux.rs` 6, `self_sandbox_linux.rs` 6 (reads only its grants, writes only `out/`/`tmp/`, cannot exec, `media.look` under the profile with lavapipe, `library.probe` granted and not), `gpu` unit 2, `fin-library` `volume.rs` +1. Run on the Linux VM (Landlock ABI 8); `cargo check --target` for Windows and macOS (`fin-sandbox`, `fin-library`; macOS with `blake3` pure)
  - Review of Tasks 1–2 (2026-11-08): roots open through `openat2` with `RESOLVE_NO_SYMLINKS|RESOLVE_NO_MAGICLINKS` (a linked parent is refused, not only a linked leaf); `validate` refuses a device rule at, under or above a denied or protected root (`DeviceOverlaps`), and a read-write device must be a character device; `restrict_self_at` is test-hooks only; escape suites accept only `EACCES`/`EXDEV` for filesystem and TCP denials (`EPERM` for the scoped signal) and the worker suite runs every denied case unsandboxed as a control. For Task 5: `agent`/`hook` have no device rules, so `/dev/null`, `/dev/tty` and `/dev/urandom` need a decision before agents go live (at ABI ≥ 5 a tty `ioctl` is refused)

a protected path is unreadable unless a read root wins, as the model says), then `file-write*` refused on directories holding an excluded path and `file-read-data` on directories holding a denied path (or a protected one, unless readable through a read root), `file-read-metadata` on the ancestors of each grant and on `/etc`, `/tmp`, `/var`; exec from read roots (not `worker`) and the agent's write roots, `process-fork` and `signal (target same-sandbox)` except `worker`, FIFOs refused for `worker`/`plugin`, `open`/`osascript`/`launchctl`/`security`/setuid helpers refused by name; net: **filtered `network-outbound` rules are not evaluated by order on macOS 27** (a filtered allow wins over a later filtered deny; a filtered deny wins over an unfiltered allow), so `agent` egress is `(allow network-outbound)` + a filtered deny of `localhost:*` and every Unix socket + filtered allows of the listener ports, the granted sockets and mDNSResponder; the Seatbelt base: `literal /`, `/System/Library`, `sysctl-read`. `macos/apply.rs`: `restrict_self` (`sandbox_init_with_parameters`) for the launcher (then `exec`; the profile never reaches argv) and the worker's self-sandbox (which may also read its own binary's ancestors' metadata: without it the media worker dies `SIGSEGV` when Metal starts); `plugin_command` rewrites a plugin to `/usr/bin/sandbox-exec -p … -D… -- <program>`. `status.rs`: macOS `Enforced {seatbelt}` where `/usr/bin/sandbox-exec` exists, else `Unavailable {need: "Seatbelt (/usr/bin/sandbox-exec)"}`; `self_apply`, the launcher and the spawner have their macOS arms; `media.capabilities.sandbox` is `worker` on macOS (the full profile). `data/macos/toolchain-base.txt` from S9; `toolchain_base_resolved` keeps a macOS link entry as itself too. **`O_PATH` equivalent on macOS:** `fin-library`'s macOS opener opens its root and directories `O_SEARCH` (it opened the root `O_RDONLY`), and `fin-shm`'s walk does too (it opened every directory `O_RDONLY`, so a sandboxed worker could not open its own `in/` files); `fin-shm`'s `sync_dir` reopens a walk descriptor for reading when `fsync` refuses it (`O_PATH`/`O_SEARCH`). The probe runs on macOS (`fork`, `mach-lookup`, `shm-open`, `sysctl`, `system-socket`, `signal-self`). - [x] Task 12: warm workers under a sandbox: per-job grants (branch `sp7c-t12`; design recorded in the plan's Task 12 before coding). As built: a warm worker's spawn request grants its own `tmp/` and `jobs/` (read-write) and, under an applied profile, its project's read roots (`FamilySpec.warmReads(job)`; `media`: the project's `sources/` and the libraries' roots, canonical), never the jobs root; `prepare` and the files are made at `<root>/<jobId>/` as for a cold job, then the warm attempt renames the directory to `<w.dir>/jobs/<jobId>/` for the helpers, the `start` and the held-files check, and back when the attempt ends, so `onSucceeded`, the cleanup and restart recovery keep one place and nothing of a job stays in the worker's directory after `ready`; a lost worker's directory is removed only once its job has left it (`Worker.visit`); a failed move is `IO_ERROR`. Pool: under an applied profile (`caps().profiles` or a self-sandboxed `hello`) a warm key is `<project>/<key>` (one warm worker per project and family, owner decision) and pre-warming is skipped (only a job names a project); `sandboxed(profile)` (warm kinds cold) only without `perJobGrants`, which covers the `worker` profile only (warm plugins stay cold); a self-sandboxed `hello` is the expected case with per-job grants; a job whose `reads` (after `prepare`) are outside its worker's roots is not started there (`WORKER_UNAVAILABLE {reason: warmReach}`, retry `fresh`, the worker stays) and its retry runs cold. Rust: `grants::worker_input` maps a warm request (`cwd` a `warm-…` directory in the jobs root with a read-write grant on its `jobs/`) to `WorkerScope::Warm {workerDir, libraryRoots}`; `SandboxSpawner::per_job_grants()` is true where the `worker` profile is applied on Linux (macOS after Task 8; Windows false until Task 10's broker grants per job: not done here, no `sandbox/broker.ts`); `test.warm` gains `probe` (paths it tries to read, an errno each, in `probes`). sp5 spec §6.3 note closed. Mutants (each fails its case): no per-project key (`a_warm_worker_reads_only_its_own_job_and_project`, `per_job_grants_admit_warm_kinds_one_worker_per_project`), no visit or no move back (`per_job_grants_admit…`), no reach check (`a_job_reading_outside_its_workers_roots_runs_cold`), `perJobGrants` ignored (`per_job_grants_admit…`), the jobs root readable in the warm profile (`a_warm_worker_reads_only_the_job_visiting_it_and_its_project`). Not separately tested: the hand-off budget of sp5 §6.3 (the existing `dispatch_p99_under_5ms_under_load` covers the hand-off, which gains one `rename` per attempt). Tests: `warm-sandbox.test.ts` 6 (real `fin-workers` under the Landlock profile with its unsandboxed control; scripted host for the pool), `warm_landlock.rs` 1 (with its control), `grants` unit +1
  - Review of Task 12 (2026-11-09): **blocker fixed:** the warm profile granted write on the worker's whole directory, so a worker could rename its `tmp/` or `jobs/` and put a link there, which `finisherd`'s `emptyTmp` and the move back then followed (deleting any directory `finisherd` can write); the profile now writes `<workerDir>/jobs` only (and `tmp/`), and `finisherd` follows neither: `emptyTmp` (now emptying `jobs/` too) refuses a `tmp/` or `jobs/` that is not a directory and loses the worker, and the move back refuses a job directory replaced by anything else (`JOB_FAILED {problem: link}`, `tampered`) and loses the worker; a failed move back loses the worker too (it may hold the job), is retried on Windows (`EPERM`/`EBUSY`/`EACCES`), and the `.prepared~` marker is removed on the way home (the worker could write `in/`, so `prepare` runs again before another attempt). `media.look`'s warm roots include the font cache (`prepare` lists it in every job's reads, so no `media.look` ran warm before); a reach miss runs cold in the same attempt (no attempt or fresh retry spent; the job's `warm` is set false); the warm key and the read roots come from one field (`projectOfJob`: `project`, else the params' `projectId`); a lost worker's directory is kept from the hourly sweep until removed (`#removing`, not separately tested: a narrow window); `per_job_grants` is Linux only until Task 8 has a Seatbelt worker profile. Mutants (each fails its case): the profile writing the whole directory (`a_warm_worker_reads_only_the_job_visiting_it_and_its_project`: renaming `tmp/` and `jobs/` succeeds), no `lstat` of the job directory (`a_link_planted_for_its_jobDir_is_never_followed`), no check in `emptyTmp` (`…_for_its_tmp_…`), the worker kept after a failed move back (`…_for_its_jobDir_…`), the reach miss failing the attempt (`a_reach_miss_runs_cold_in_the_same_attempt`), no font cache (`media_warm_reads_…`). Not changed (N5): `mediaWarmReads` resolves its roots synchronously, once per warm worker spawned. Tests: `warm-sandbox.test.ts` 10, `warm_landlock.rs` 1 (+ rename probes)
- [x] Task 9: Windows AppContainer, job objects and WER (branch `sp7c-t9`). As built: `fin-sandbox` `windows/` (`capability`, `plan`, `grants`: pure, tested on every OS; `appcontainer`, `job`, `desktop`, `apply`, `launcher`, `wer`, `token`, `acl`, `sid`), `fin-sandbox-exec` on Windows (starts the program in its project's container inside a kill-on-close job on a private desktop, stays its parent, relays the exit code; the request on an inherited handle or C runtime descriptor), the Windows `SandboxSpawner` arm (worker and plugin: the security-capabilities attribute, a private desktop, the container UI limits; the worker keeps its self-sandbox request, which checks its token), `fin-jobhost` `PlatformCommand.desktop`, `HookInput.timeoutMs` (the launcher's wall time), the Node guard (`data/windows/node-guard.cjs`; flags and guard on Node's command line and in `NODE_OPTIONS`), `windows::grants::{agent, hook}` (the broker's ACE table, enumerated against the policy). `fastfail_dies_without_wer` asserts no WerFault window and no report (Decision 22). Tests on the Windows host, non-elevated: `container_windows` 15, `node_windows` 5, `spawner_windows` 1, lib 23; fin-workers (test-hooks) and fin-jobs suites green. 12 mutants on Windows and 3 on Linux each fail a test (commit `docs(7C)`). libuv: fixed upstream in v1.53.0, Node does not bundle it yet; the owner's Node request is prepared in `archive/s9/windows/libuv-appcontainer-pipes/`.
  - Review of Task 9 (2026-11-09): **B1 fixed (verified on the host):** an AppContainer token's default DACL admits its container SID, so every process of a container could open every other one with `PROCESS_ALL_ACCESS` (sessions and hooks of a project, a worker family's cold jobs across projects, a plugin's calls); each sandboxed process now gets an explicit DACL (`SYSTEM`, the user, its own session/hook/job/call capability) at creation, and while suspended its token's default DACL is set the same way, so its threads and children get it too (`fin_jobhost::process_dacl`, `PlatformCommand.dacl` for the host's spawns; `processes_of_other_sessions_grant_nothing`, `children_of_other_sessions_grant_nothing`). **B2 fixed:** the project was granted to the shared container SID, so a Codex session's or a hook's grant let a Claude Code session read its denied project files; the project and libraries are now read through a view capability named after the folder and the denied paths inside it (`capability::project_view`; `another_harness_grant_does_not_open_claude_codes_denied_files`); the container SID gets no file grant. **B3 fixed:** without standard handles the launcher passed every inheritable handle (no handle list, inheritance on): inheritance is now off then, and the raw-handle request is made non-inheritable (`only_the_standard_handles_reach_the_container`). Should-fix done: the shared station admits containers with read-attributes, global atoms and `READ_CONTROL` only (less fails their start, measured), a station another program made is given that entry, the desktop admits its container with object rights only (`the_station_and_desktop_admit_only_what_they_must`); worker and plugin `TEMP` is their own (`LOCALAPPDATA` in their private temp); a Node plugin host gets the Node flags; violation lines go on the request channel (stderr only when it is one-way); the backstop counts reads and writes only (a stuck libuv retry loop counts as "other" I/O: measured, `wall_time_reports_node_pipes_for_a_node_without_the_guard`, `wall_time_reports_wall_time_for_a_busy_program`); the guard file is written by rename and only when it changed; the WER watchers match any new report (names are shortened) and the container crash test has an unconfined control that WerFault sees; UI-limit bits asserted literally; the Node suite finds `node.exe` on `PATH`. Guard: a probe timeout is no longer cached, `exec`/`execFile` always count as piped. Kept as follow-ups: `SetProcessWindowStation` is process-wide during desktop creation in `finisherd` (a concurrent spawn without `lpDesktop` could land on the hidden station for that instant); the spawner's per-container desktops live as long as the server; the worker's token self-check checks "is an AppContainer", not the planned SIDs; no test starts a real worker or plugin through the host's spawn path with the security-capabilities attribute (Task 16's suite under `READY`); container names hash the folder string as given (another spelling gives another container: fails closed). The plugin arm's active-process limit was already set (finding withdrawn).

- [x] Task 10: the ACL broker and per-project pipe SIDs (branches `sp7c-t10`, then `sp7c-t9`; Windows host, non-elevated, 2026-11-09: `acl_broker` (test-hooks) and `pipe_server` suites, `acl-broker.test.ts` and `pipe.test.ts` pass; fin-sandbox, fin-workers (test-hooks), fin-jobs and clippy `-D warnings` for fin-sandbox, fin-jobhost, fin-workers and fin-node pass there too). As built: `crates/fin-node/src/acl_broker/{mod, wal, apply, sweep, protect}.rs` (napi-free, `tests/acl_broker.rs` includes it): grants `{path, sid, mask, inherit, owner}` as allow-only ACEs (`apply::Platform`: `fin_sandbox::windows::acl` on Windows, `Noop` elsewhere, so the log runs on every OS); each operation appended to `<server-data>/run/acl-broker.wal` and fsynced, applied, marked done; replay at open completes an incomplete revoke and an incomplete grant of a live owner, else revokes it, then rewrites the log (a torn last line dropped, a damaged one refused); `sweep(live)` revokes every grant of an owner not live and retries failed revokes (`static` is always live: toolchain, project folder, sign-in store, granted once); one operation at a time on a path, its ancestors and descendants (the S8 lost-grant race, and inheritance rewriting descendants); one owner per `(path, sid)` (`INVALID_OP {reason: aclOwned}`), a covered re-grant is a no-op (no log, no apply); the log is rewritten past 2 × live + pending + 256 lines. **Protected (before the log, `protect.rs`):** malformed (relative, `.`/`..`, a mask beyond modify `0x1301BF`, a SID or owner that is not one: `INVALID_ARGS`); server data and every folder holding it; inside server data only `agents/<id>/{tmp,provider,localappdata}` (any), `agents/<id>/{credential.json,settings,git}` (read), `providers/<h>/home/<store>`, `jobs/<x>`, `hooks/<x>` (any), `plugins/{store,models}/<x>`, `fonts`, `cache` (read); a write mask on a project folder (holds `finisher.project.json`) or its `.finisher`, `assets`, `finisher.project.json`, `finisher.rules.json` (`PATH_NOT_ALLOWED {reason: aclProtected}`); a missing path `NOT_FOUND`; checked on the path and its canonical form. Test hook (`test-hooks` only): `FIN_TEST_ACL_CRASH=<grant|revoke>:<afterAppend|afterApply>` aborts there. napi (`sandbox_acl.rs`): `aclGrant`, `aclRevoke(serverData, owner, path?)`, `aclSweep(serverData, live)` on the libuv pool (one broker per canonical server data), `sandboxAgentGrants`/`sandboxHookGrants` (the coordinator's `fin_sandbox::windows::grants` table, copied from `sp7c-t9`; `AclGrant`, `AclTrustee`, `AclScope` generated into core-types), `sandboxProjectContainer`, `sandboxContainerSid` (Windows; `null` elsewhere), `aclGrantsForTest`. finisherd: `sandbox/broker.ts` (`aclBroker`, a no-op off Windows; `grantTable`: `static` entries under the static owner, others under `session:<id>`, a missing path skipped); `main.ts` sweeps with no owner live right after the server lock, before the job engine and every listener; `agent/sandbox.ts` on Windows (launcher mode) allows the project's container SID on the pipe (`PipeServer.allow` through `pipe.ts`'s new `onAllow`, once per SID) and grants the agent table before any process (and an `extraRead` input's table), revoking the session's grants when its scope closes or a grant fails; `ensureSessionDirs` loses its `grant` option (the masks come from the table). Mutants (each fails its case): no path lock (`two_concurrent_grants_on_one_directory_both_survive`), equal paths only (`a_directory_and_one_inside_it_are_never_changed_at_once`), no compaction (`the_log_stays_bounded`), an incomplete grant always completed (`a_crash_after_the_grant_is_{logged,applied}_is_completed`), an incomplete revoke left (`a_crash_during_a_revoke_is_completed`), no crash hook (the 3 crash tests), the sweep ignoring liveness (`the_sweep_revokes_the_grants_of_owners_gone`), each protected rule (server data and ancestors, inside server data, project state, the mask) (`protected_roots_are_refused_before_the_log`), no torn-tail tolerance (`a_torn_last_line_…`), two owners of a pair (`one_owner_per_pair_…`); finisherd: no revoke at scope close, no grant before the start, no direct pipe allow (`grants_the_agent_table_before_any_process_and_revokes_it_at_scope_close`), every entry under the session owner and no container callback (`static_entries_are_kept_…`), the wrapper active off Windows (`is_a_no_op_off_windows`). Equivalent (survives): a failed grant marked done before its rollback revoke (replay gives the same at every point). Tests: `tests/acl_broker.rs` 9 + 3 crash (`--features test-hooks`) on every OS, `tests/acl_broker/windows.rs` 3 (real grant then revoke leaves the DACL as before; two concurrent real grants both present; `PipeCore.allow(<container SID>)` admits project X's container, and project Y's is refused by `AccessCheck` on its waiting child's impersonation token against the pipe's descriptor, with X's as the control: no escape attempt, owner decision 2026-11-08), `acl-broker.test.ts` 11. Not done: hook grants (no Windows hook launch in finisherd yet), per-job grants for workers (Task 12's Windows side; `per_job_grants` stays false there), session directories made with the ACE in their creating descriptor (granted after creation instead: about 1 ms on an empty directory, S9), the broker reading the DACL back before skipping a covered re-grant (it trusts its own log), a test that `main.ts` sweeps before listening. **Review of Task 10** (`fix(fin-node): Task 10 review`): **B1** `sandboxToolchainBase()` expands `%SystemRoot%`/`%ProgramFiles%` (`fin_sandbox::data::toolchain_base_windows`, pure; `toolchain_base_present` drops missing roots) and the Windows apply treats a read grant whose access the DACL already gives (`ALL APPLICATION PACKAGES`, inherited or explicit, or an explicit ACE of the grant's SID; `fin_sandbox::windows::dacl::covers`, pure, and `acl::admits`) as done with no DACL change; a root that admits no container and cannot be re-ACLed fails naming it; `the_real_grant_tables_pass_the_protect_rules` runs the real `grants::{agent_with_extra, hook}` tables with this OS's toolchain list through the broker. **1** a grant completes any pending revoke of its pair first (under the path lock), and completing a revoke whose pair was granted again later (replay, sweep) re-applies that grant. **2** `SessionGrants` (`agent/sandbox.ts`) counts a session's open scopes: its grants are revoked when the last closes, and a scope opening during that revoke waits for it (`runtime-engine.ts` still drops a replaced instance before awaiting its close; the count makes that safe). **3** static records are dropped at open (the next launch grants again; granting merges) and a covered static re-grant reads the DACL back (`Apply::holds`) and re-applies when the ACE is gone. **4** `extraRead` is granted to the session's capability under `session:<id>` (`grants::agent_with_extra`; `sandboxAgentGrants(input, extraRead?)`). **5** replay, `sweep` and `revoke` go on past a failed revoke (left pending; `SweepReport.failed`; `aclSweep` rejects once with `IO_ERROR` after all others are done) and `main.ts` logs it and starts (`sweepAtStart`). **6** a path is canonicalised once on entry and that form is checked, locked, recorded and applied; a path that cannot be resolved is refused, and on Windows only `Disk`/`VerbatimDisk` prefixes are accepted (UNC refused). **7** a failed rewrite of the log is a warning (`Broker::warnings`, stderr), written to a temp file first (`write_temp`) and renamed over the log while the old handle stays until the rename succeeds. - [x] Task 11 follow-up: the `setsid` escape closed on Linux (plan Decision 3 amendment, 2026-11-09; branch `sp7c-reaper`). As built: `fin-sandbox-exec` no longer `exec`s the program on Linux: `fin_sandbox::linux::reaper` makes it a child subreaper with a parent-death signal (`SIGRTMIN`, checked against `getppid` because the kernel sends it on the parent thread's exit), forks, and confines the child (own process group, session cgroup, signal mask restored, sockets hidden, Landlock, `RLIMIT_NOFILE`, death signal `SIGKILL`, `exec`); the unconfined launcher forwards `TERM`/`INT`/`QUIT`/`USR1`/`USR2`/`WINCH`/`CONT`/`ALRM`/`TSTP`/`TTIN`/`TTOU` to the program's group and, when the program exits, on `SIGHUP` and when its parent dies, kills the group, `cgroup.kill`s the session cgroup and kills each child until none is left, then exits with the program's code or re-raises its signal. Optional cgroup layer: `<own>/fin-session-<pid>` where the launcher's cgroup v2 is writable, has `cgroup.kill` and holds only its parent and the parent's children (`FIN_CGROUPS=off` skips it). `finisherd` `agent/sandbox.ts`: a `SIGKILL` of a process under the Linux launcher becomes `SIGHUP`, then `SIGKILL` of its group after `REAP_GRACE_MS` (2 s). Tests: `crates/fin-sandbox/tests/reaper_linux.rs` (5: program exit, hang-up and parent death each take a `setsid` grandchild, unconfined and confined; `SIGINT` reaches the program and its exit 5 passes through; the session cgroup in a `systemd-run --user -p Delegate=yes` unit, skipped with a notice elsewhere, a failure under `FIN_REQUIRE_CGROUP=1`); `agent-sandbox.test.ts` `a_process_that_leaves_its_group_goes_with_the_session` (was `…outlives_the_session`; direct mode kept as the control) and the scope-close case under the real launcher now takes a `setsid` grandchild. Task 5's launcher tests unchanged and passing. Mutants: per-child kill removed → the reaper tests fail (or hang); cgroup join removed → the cgroup test fails; `finisherd` keeping `SIGKILL` → the scope-close test fails. Residuals: below Landlock ABI 6 the program can kill its launcher (its orphans then live on unless the cgroup takes them); an empty session cgroup is left if the launcher itself is `SIGKILL`ed; direct mode keeps the escape (no sandbox there). **macOS owes (a Task 8 follow-up; Task 8 landed in parallel and left the macOS launcher `exec`ing):** no subreaper or `PDEATHSIG` there and orphans go to `launchd`, so either Seatbelt refuses `setsid`/`setpgid` (to verify) or the launcher tracks descendants while alive (`proc_listchildpids` or `kqueue` `NOTE_FORK`) and kills them at session end, parent death by `kqueue` `NOTE_EXIT`; until then `finisherd`'s `SIGHUP` mapping is Linux-only. Windows: the job object already covers it (Task 9).
  - Review (2026-11-09, fresh reviewer, no blockers): **fixed:** an inherited `SIGCHLD` `SIG_IGN` (it survives `exec`) let the kernel reap the program unseen and the launcher never ended; `prepare` resets it, and no child left also ends the session (`an_ignored_sigchld_is_not_inherited_by_the_reaper`, red before the fix); the group kill after the program is reaped is skipped (its id could name another process's group); a failed `fork` removes the session cgroup; cgroup detection also requires a writable `cgroup.subtree_control` (as `fin-jobhost`'s); Task 5's test comments no longer say the launcher `exec`s; every reaper test spawn has a 30 s deadline so a regression fails rather than hangs; `a_launcher_that_ignores_the_hang_up_is_killed_after_the_grace` covers `finisherd`'s `REAP_GRACE_MS` fallback. **Not changed:** `the_parents_death_ends_the_session` also passes on the 1 s `getppid` poll alone, so it does not prove the death signal by itself; writability cannot tell a `Delegate=yes` unit from another unit of the user's manager (no delegation xattr is readable there), the same residual as `fin-jobhost`'s detection; a launcher `SIGKILL`ed by the fallback leaves its empty `fin-session-<pid>` cgroup.
- [x] Task 11 on Windows, hook and per-job worker grants, a real worker and plugin through the job host's Windows spawn path (branch `sp7c-winfu`, 2026-11-09). As built:
  - **Session sandbox on Windows** (`agent/session-dirs.ts`, `agent/sandbox.ts`): no `fchmod`; a session directory's name is `lstat`ed and anything but a real directory (a junction, a link, a file a container planted) removed, never followed; a new one inherits server data's protected owner-only DACL; an existing one keeps its DACL (it holds the broker's live grants of the session's other open scopes), so a write root a container deleted is re-made without its grant for the rest of the session (only the session loses it); an `EEXIST` race accepts a real directory. `sessionDirs(root, id, platform?)` spells paths for the platform named (pure; `ensureSessionDirs` keeps the host's spelling). Workspace and shared directory resolved after `mkdir`; a process starts only in one of them (above). Inside the container Windows roots `LOCALAPPDATA` at `<session>/localappdata/Packages/<container>/AC` (`TEMP` under it).
  - **`fin` reads its session credential in the container:** the broker's read-only ACE for the session capability on `agents/<id>/credential.json` made `fin` refuse it (`credentialUnsafe`, found by the new Windows case); `fin`'s Windows privacy check accepts exactly that SID with a read mask (`auth::session_reader`, `private_windows::problems(file, reader)`; `fin` gains `fin-sandbox` as a Windows dependency for the derivation).
  - **Job host grants through the broker** (plan Decisions 18, 19): `fin_sandbox::windows::job_grants` (pure): `worker` (own capability: temp and `out/` modify, `in/` and sources or library roots read; family container: caches and the program, static), `plugin` (call capability: `in/`, `out/`, temp, and the host's other reads; container: program, package, models, plugin data, static; toolchain capability only on `runtime`/`onnxRuntime`), `visit` (a job in `<w.dir>/jobs/` to the warm worker's capability). `SandboxSpawner::acl_grants(req, program)` (empty where the profile passes, the `prepare` refusal otherwise), fin-node `JobHost.aclGrants`, `sandboxVisitGrant`. `EngineHost.spawn` grants the table before the spawn under `proc:<start>-<n>`, revokes it at the exit and on a failed spawn, and waits for running revokes first (a retry grants the same pairs; one owner per pair); `warm-run.ts` grants a visiting job after its move in and revokes it before its move back (`EngineHost.visit`, `job:<id>`; a failure is `SANDBOX_UNAVAILABLE` and the job goes home). `per_job_grants` is true on Windows where the `worker` profile applies. A warm worker's `jobs/` is **not** granted: Windows recomputes inherited entries at every ACL write, so an inheritable worker ACE above a job would be copied onto it by the visit grant and leave with it (review B1). `main.ts` hands the broker to the job engine.
  - **`fin-jobhost` fix:** `WinAttribute` gains `size` (the attribute's `cbSize`; the job host passed the whole self-contained `SECURITY_CAPABILITIES` buffer, so every container spawn through the job host failed `ERROR_INVALID_PARAMETER`; found by `host_sandbox_windows.rs`); a size beyond the value is refused.
  - **Hook grants: not wired**, because nothing in `finisherd` launches a hook yet (7F does; `sandboxHookGrants` and `grantTable` are ready, owner `call:<id>` per Task 10's naming).
  - Tests (Decision 27: configuration, allowed paths and `AccessCheck`, no escape attempt): `agent-sandbox.test.ts` per-OS (POSIX cases `skipIf(win32)`; Windows: private session directories, a planted junction removed, and `windows_agent_runs_in_its_container_with_the_sessions_grants`: the working directory, the container's `LOCALAPPDATA`/`TEMP`, `fin whoami` as the agent over the pipe, the broker's grants and their revoke at the end, nothing granted on server data, `auth/`, `blobs/` or the other project); `acl-grants.test.ts` 9 (recording broker, every OS); `warm-sandbox.test.ts` Windows variant (allowed probes, each live worker's grants through the real broker; junctions instead of symlinks); `job_grants` unit 5, `spawner_acl.rs` 4; `crates/fin-workers/tests/host_sandbox_windows.rs` 2 (a real `fin-workers test` worker and `testplugin` plugin under `SandboxSpawner::new` with `FIN_TEST_SANDBOX_READY=worker,plugin`: token container and capabilities read from the pid, the leaf limits, desktop and process DACL `prepare` set, `test.files` on its grants, denials by `AccessCheck`); `host_windows.rs` +1 (oversized attribute); `fin` auth +2. ## Windows run follow-ups (2026-11-08, `xwin-fixes`)

- [x] 4A: `fin` tests fail `ENGINE_BUSY {operation: "backup (auto)"}` (exit 5) on Windows when `cargo test -p fin` runs alone: the first-open daily backup holds the engine while parallel tests write, and `VACUUM INTO` plus fsync is slower there. A user's first command after a start can meet the same; ties to the 4A follow-up "the store mutex across folder I/O" (take the backup outside the engine's lock, or let writes wait for it)
  - Fixed (2026-11-08, `fin-node::auto`, spec §3.8/§3.10): automatic work starts only after 30 s with no short call (or 10 min after it fell due); the daily backup runs off the store on its own connection and thread (a WAL snapshot; `quick_check` before the no-replace rename; cancelled by any long call and retaken later); compaction runs one step per tick under the mutex. Neither takes the store out, so neither can cause `ENGINE_BUSY`. The test-build default that turned automatic work off (`setAutoWorkForTest`) is gone; tests set the quiet time (`setAutoIdleForTest`) where they need the work to run. New tests: 50 short calls during a backup due at open (vitest), a long call cancelling a running backup, the quiet-engine rule, and fin-store's `backup_job` (commits during the copy, cancels, truncated files, a process killed mid-backup). To re-check on Windows: `cargo test -p fin` alone
- [ ] Windows vitest under the full suite: `control_store_budget` (median), `dispatch_p99_under_5ms_under_load` (p50), `engine.test` event loop: timing bounds that only hold unloaded; all pass alone (integration's `b876ef9` now gives every test 30 s unless `FIN_STRICT_TIMING=1`, which covers Windows' slower process start)

## Load-sensitive timing tests (patches for the owning branches)

Pattern: statistic + generous bound, strict bound only under `FIN_STRICT_TIMING=1`. Done for `engine.test.ts` "event loop keeps ticking" on sp4c-edit-breadth. The two below live on other branches and need the same treatment there.

- [ ] **`reader_never_waits_for_writer`** (`apps/finisherd/test/library/threads.test.ts`, home/sp5d-t7b; failed at 20.2 ms vs 20 ms). The writer spins 800 ms, so a reader that really waits for it takes about 700 ms. Replace `expect(Math.max(...times)).toBeLessThan(20)` with:
  ```ts
  const sorted = [...times].sort((a, b) => a - b);
  expect(sorted[Math.floor(sorted.length / 2)]).toBeLessThan(20); // median
  expect(sorted.filter((t) => t > 20).length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(2); // at most 2 of the 10 searches
  expect(sorted[sorted.length - 1]).toBeLessThan(process.env.FIN_STRICT_TIMING === "1" ? 20 : 250);
  ```
  Keep `expect(spinning).toBe(true)`. Prove with a mutant that makes `reader.search` take the writer's lock (it must fail on the 250 ms bound or the median).
- [x] **`diagnostics_project_never_then_default`** (`crates/fin/tests/cli_diagnostics_online.rs`, home/sp5a-t3, fn at ~539; times out under load). The positive waits go through `wait()` with a fixed 15 s deadline. Make the deadline `Duration::from_secs(env_secs("FIN_TEST_WAIT_SECS", 60))` (60 s default; `fn env_secs(k, d) -> u64 { std::env::var(k).ok().and_then(|v| v.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(d) }`). The failure branch stays: `assert!(want(&found), "timed out: ...")` after the deadline. Leave the 400 ms sleep before "no new ones are written" alone (it is a negative check and load only makes it weaker, never flaky); keep each `fin` call's own timeout, if any, scaled by the same value. Done on `sp5a-t5`: `wait()` takes `FIN_TEST_WAIT_SECS` (default 60 s); `fin` calls there have no timeout of their own to scale; the 400 ms negative check is unchanged.
